### [185] [LF-GICP: Parameter-Free Degeneracy-Aware LiDAR Odometry via a Voxel-Normal Localizability Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19522)
*Eunsoo Im*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需参数调优的LiDAR里程计退化处理方法LF-GICP，通过正则化无关的体素法向局部可观测性场及其统计量，在退化环境中实现鲁棒且高精度的定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扫描到地图的LiDAR里程计在几何退化环境（如隧道、走廊）中沿不可观测轴产生无界漂移，现有退化处理需要针对特定环境调整参数，缺乏通用性。

Method: 提出LF-GICP方法，利用正则化无关的体素法向局部可观测性场，并提取两个统计量：归一化分数f0检测方向各向异性，绝对每体素质量λ0区分信息缺失（隧道）与信息稀释（密集开放场景）。通过时间中值门控触发Fisher信息对应加权，并采用固定规则校准参数。

Result: 在KITTI上达到最低相对平移误差（0.865%），在GEODE隧道和MulRan上优于现有方法，在HeLiPR上领先均值，且无需重新调参即可泛化到四种传感器类型。

Conclusion: LF-GICP是一种参数无关且通用的退化处理方法，能有效缓解退化环境中的漂移；同时实验表明笔直均匀隧道沿其轴线在LiDAR-only注册中仍不可观测。

Abstract: Scan-to-map LiDAR odometry drifts unboundedly along the unobservable axes of geometrically degenerate environments like tunnels and corridors, and existing degeneracy handling requires environment-specific parameter tuning. This paper presents a parameter-free approach. We show that in voxelized GICP the Gauss--Newton (GN) Hessian masks translational degeneracy, because covariance regularization keeps the translation block artificially well-conditioned. We bypass this with a regularization-free voxel-normal localizability field and two of its statistics: a normalized fraction $f_0$ detecting directional anisotropy, and an absolute per-voxel mass $λ_0$ distinguishing information absence (tunnels) from dilution (dense open scenes). A temporal-median gate combines both to trigger Fisher-information correspondence weighting. Calibrated once by fixed rules on two short sequences and then frozen, LF-GICP achieves the lowest KITTI relative translation error ($0.865\%$) under an identical evaluation protocol against re-run baselines, outperforms them on GEODE tunnels and MulRan, leads the HeLiPR mean, and generalizes across four sensor types without re-tuning. We further demonstrate empirically that straight, uniform tunnels remain unobservable along their axis for LiDAR-only registration.

</details>


### [186] [Multimodal Trajectory Planning for Surface Vehicles using Turning Circle-based Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19537)
*Changyu Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无引导路径的多模态轨迹规划框架，用于自主水面船舶在动态环境中的运动规划。该方法结合模型预测控制（MPC）和基于转弯圆的控制屏障函数（TC-CBF），通过考虑船舶的非完整运动学和有限转弯能力，生成左转和右转两种避免模式，从而避免局部最小值和死锁问题，并在模拟中表现出更高的成功率、更少的安全违规和更小的残余违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于欧氏距离的控制屏障函数（ED-CBF）仅根据接近程度评估安全性，忽略了水面船舶的非完整运动和有限转弯能力，导致在多模态规划中需要全局引导路径，且容易陷入局部最小值和死锁问题。

Method: 提出了TC-CBF，基于转弯圆的几何构型识别可行避碰区域，生成左转和右转两种避免模式，并将避免方向直接嵌入安全约束中，与MPC集成，实现无引导路径的多模态轨迹规划。

Result: 在多种交通密度的模拟中，与单模式基线相比，提出的方法实现了更高的成功率、更少的安全违规次数和更小的残余违规。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能够有效利用船舶的转弯特性，避免局部最小值和死锁，同时保持计算效率，适用于动态环境中的自主水面船舶轨迹规划。

Abstract: This paper presents a guide path-free multimodal trajectory planning framework for autonomous surface vehicles operating in dynamic environments. The proposed method integrates model predictive control (MPC) with a turning circle-based control barrier function (TC-CBF). Unlike conventional Euclidean distance-based CBFs (ED-CBFs), which evaluate safety solely based on proximity, the TC-CBF accounts for the nonholonomic motion and finite turning capability of a surface vehicle. Its geometric formulation identifies feasible avoidance regions according to the vehicle's turning circles and generates distinct left- and right-turning avoidance modes. These modes allow the optimization solver to explore and select topologically different trajectories without relying on globally planned guide paths, as required by many conventional multimodal planning approaches. By embedding the avoidance direction directly into the safety constraint, the proposed framework alleviates the local-minimum and deadlock problems of single-mode MPC while maintaining computational efficiency. Extensive simulations involving multiple moving vessels demonstrate that the proposed method achieves higher success rates, fewer safety violations, and smaller residual violations than single-mode baselines across all tested traffic densities.

</details>


### [187] [HiTac-WAM: A Hierarchical Tactile World Action Model for Contact-Rich Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19574)
*Chao Xue,Chaofan Zhang,Wenxuan Ma,Guocai Yao,Shaowei Cui,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HiTac-WAM，一种分层触觉世界动作模型，通过预测接触状态、3D变形场和滑移风险来指导机器人操作规划，显著提升真实机器人任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉感知的世界模型将未来触觉表示为图像或潜在流，未建模触觉状态的层次物理依赖关系，导致预测不准确和规划效果差。

Method: HiTac-WAM将未来触觉状态预测分解为接触状态、3D变形场和滑移风险，形成有向层次结构，每级以停止梯度信号为条件；采用有向注意力掩码使触觉查询关注视频-动作上下文，并用于候选动作排序和基于预测-观测差异的重新规划。

Result: 接触F1为0.921；相比仅变形预测器，3D位移L2误差降低17.6%；相比仅滑移预测器，滑移AUPRC提升60.4%；在芯片抓取、黑板擦除和USB插入任务中，平均成功率从31.1%提升至61.1%，完整系统达72.2%。

Conclusion: 层次化触觉状态预测显著提升世界模型的物理一致性和机器人操作成功率，验证了结构分解的有效性。

Abstract: World action models jointly predict future visual observations and actions, whereas existing tactile-aware variants typically represent future touch as an image or latent stream without modeling the physical dependencies that organize tactile states hierarchically. We present HiTac-WAM, a hierarchical tactile world action model that forecasts a sequence of future tactile states for each candidate action chunk before execution. The forecast factorizes into contact state, a 3D deformation field, and slip risk, organized as a directed hierarchy in which each downstream stage is conditioned on stop-gradient signals from preceding stages. A directed attention mask allows tactile queries to attend to the video-action context of each candidate while preventing video and action queries from attending to tactile tokens. For planning, HiTac-WAM ranks candidate action chunks using tactile forecasts and task-progress estimates. For execution, the selected tactile forecast is retained as a reference; persistent discrepancies between predicted and observed tactile states trigger corrective replanning. HiTac-WAM achieves a mean contact F1 of 0.921; under matched training budgets, the directed hierarchy reduces 3D displacement L2 error by 17.6% relative to the deformation-only predictor and improves slip AUPRC by 60.4% relative to the slip-only predictor. Across chip grasping, blackboard erasing, and USB insertion, selection guided by the hierarchical forecasts increases the average real-robot success rate from 31.1% to 61.1%, while the full system attains 72.2%.

</details>


### [188] [OrthoSkillVLA: Continual Skill Learning via Gradient-Informed Skill Subspace Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19589)
*Jiaqi Wang,Zhou Fang,Qiongfeng Shi,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OrthoSkillVLA，一种针对预训练VLA模型的参数高效持续学习框架，通过分离子空间约束和特征感知MoE解码器，在不重放演示的情况下缓解灾难性遗忘，同时保持先前技能并学习新技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的视觉-语言-动作模型在顺序适应不同技能时会干扰已有技能的表征和速度映射，导致灾难性遗忘。现有方法（如架构隔离或统一子空间约束）存在推理开销大或约束不匹配VLA内部组件差异的问题。

Method: 分析VLA内部组件（VLM和ActionHead）的不同角色，对VLM和ActionHead施加分离的子空间约束，以保留语义表征并保护速度模式；对输出层引入轻量级特征感知MoE解码器，为每个技能分配紧凑专家，并用训练无关的路由器根据特征空间亲和度选择专家。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界评估中，OrthoSkillVLA相比现有方法能更好地保留先前技能并学习新技能，消融实验验证了各组件设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模VLA内部表征异质性，分离约束与特征感知路由可有效解决持续技能学习中的灾难性遗忘，且无需演示重放，为实际部署提供高效方案。

Abstract: Pretrained Vision-Language-Action models provide a strong foundation for robot learning, but sequentially adapting them to diverse skills can perturb the representations and velocity mappings used by previous skills, leading to catastrophic forgetting. Architecture-based approaches improve retention by isolating skills but lead to increased inference footprint. Recent subspace-constrained methods restrict parameter updates in an orthogonal subspace to minimize interference but impose a unified constraint on the entire model. We analyze the distinct roles of internal VLA components and identify two VLA-specific challenges. First, the VLM maintains broad semantic representations, making it vulnerable to capacity exhaustion, whereas the ActionHead refines semantics into localized velocity patterns that are highly sensitive to perturbations. Second, the final velocity decoder serves as a readout layer. Freezing it forms an output-stage expressivity bottleneck, while updating it risks overwriting previous velocity mappings. To this end, we propose OrthoSkillVLA, a parameter-efficient framework for continual skill learning in pretrained VLA models without demonstration replay. Given the representation heterogeneity, we impose separate subspace constraints on the VLM and ActionHead, preserving reusable semantic capacity while protecting localized velocity patterns. For the output layer, we introduce a lightweight feature-aware MoE decoder, where each skill is allocated a compact expert and a training-free router selects the expert according to feature-space affinity. Extensive simulated and real-world evaluations, together with ablations, demonstrate that OrthoSkillVLA better preserves prior skills while acquiring new ones.

</details>


### [189] [What Matters for Latent Actions in Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19613)
*Xizhou Bu,Qingda Hu,Lei Zhou,Lingfeng Zhang,Yingbo Tang,Zihao Liu,Xinyi Tao,Zhiqiang Ma,Qingqiu Huang,Chufeng Tang,Hongbo Wang,Jing Zhang,Jiayi Ma,Hangjun Ye,Wei Li,Xiaoshuai Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次对机器人操作中的潜在动作模型（LAM）进行了全面实证研究，统一了代表性方法，系统评估了41种设计选择，并发现微调VLM主干网络结合潜在动作为下游策略学习提供了更强的初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LAM研究碎片化，现有方法在不一致的实验设置下孤立评估不同设计选择，难以确定真正决定下游操作性能的因素。

Method: 在统一的自编码框架下，系统研究41种LAM设计选择（涵盖潜在动作建模范式、学习目标与正则化、以及潜在动作集成策略），并考察四种代理指标评估其预测下游性能的能力，在三个基准和真实机器人任务上验证。

Result: 微调VLM主干网络结合潜在动作显著提升了下游策略学习的初始化性能，并在多个基准和真实任务中取得一致优势。

Conclusion: 潜在动作模型的设计选择对下游性能有显著影响，微调VLM主干网络是提升操作性能的关键因素。

Abstract: Latent Action Models (LAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for enabling robot learning to leverage large-scale unlabeled videos through latent actions that serve as compact surrogates for physical actions. Despite rapid progress, research on LAM remains highly fragmented, with existing methods evaluating different design choices in isolation under inconsistent experimental settings, making it difficult to identify the factors that truly determine downstream robotic manipulation performance. In this work, we present the first comprehensive empirical study of latent action learning for robotic manipulation. We unify representative LAM methods within a common autoencoding framework and systematically investigate 41 LAM design choices across three dimensions, including latent action modeling paradigms, learning objectives and regularization methods, and latent action integration strategies. We further examine four proxy metrics for evaluating latent action quality and assess their ability to reliably predict downstream robotic manipulation performance. Extensive experiments on three widely used benchmarks provide strong empirical evidence that fine-tuning vision-language model (VLM) backbones with latent actions provides a stronger initialization for downstream policy learning, with further validation on real-world robot manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [190] [The Verification Gap in Networked Physical AI: A Post-Semantic Communication Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19593)
*Shunsuke Saruwatari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种后语义通信框架，用于解决网络物理AI中提案与物理执行之间的验证差距，通过区分证据传输与协调，并验证了框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络物理AI中，即使提案在语义上有效，也可能因缺乏及时、有界或权威的证据而无法执行，这种不匹配被称为验证差距。

Method: 提出了后语义通信框架，包括应用声明的证据需求、证据记录、验证路径、证据充分性与授权最终化的分离，以及运行时门控。通过有限状态机检查评估器一致性，并通过受控通信研究分析不同最终化模式下的行为。

Result: 揭示了最终化者依赖的不对称性：发送方最终化的反馈通过证据传输扩展可达性，而接收方最终化的反馈通过协调抑制冗余传输，直到损失、延迟、新鲜度和截止时间成本促使选择单向通信。

Conclusion: 框架为物理AI研究提供了统一的报告模式，有助于未来量化研究，并强调了证据管理与授权分离的重要性。

Abstract: A task-effective proposal is not yet a justified physical action. In networked Physical AI, a proposal may be understood while valid, timely, proposal-bound evidence or the authority required to finalize an action remains unavailable. We call this mismatch the verification gap and introduce a Post-Semantic Communication Framework for the systems interface between proposal formation and physical execution. The framework begins with application-declared evidence requirements, represents qualifying observations as evidence records, validates supporting and conflicting records through one path, and separates evidence sufficiency from authorized finalization and a downstream runtime gate. It further distinguishes evidence transfer, which can enlarge the record set reachable by a finalizer, from evidence coordination, which can suppress transmission around records already held at the finalization endpoint. Finite-state framework checks verify that the evaluator implements the declared distinctions consistently. Under the declared model, the controlled communication study exposes a finalizer-dependent asymmetry: sender-finalized Feedback uses evidence transfer to expand evidence reachability throughout the feasible plotted region, whereas receiver-finalized Feedback uses coordination to suppress redundant payload until loss, latency, freshness, and deadline costs shift selection to One-way. Finally, an episode-level reporting schema defines common denominators for future measured Physical-AI studies.

</details>


### [191] [Magnetically Self-Sealed MR Haptic Actuator With PWM-Based Excitation and High-Fidelity Torque Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19635)
*Dong Qiang,Tian Yuan,Song Yang,Kequan Xia,Thomas Reddyhoff,Yikun Zhang,Cheng Cheng,Min Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于磁流变液（MRF）的集成触觉系统，实现了高精度、高稳定性的扭矩渲染，并通过实验验证了其在长期运行中的可靠性和性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRF执行器在触觉反馈中具有紧凑和快速可控的优势，但实际应用中需要解决流体密封、低迟滞激励、精确扭矩控制和稳定长期运行等挑战。

Method: 采用磁静力学仿真优化磁路布局，结合PWM低迟滞操作和非线性模型（随扭矩变化方向和速度调整），并设计融合前馈、迟滞补偿、PI反馈和滑模校正的实时控制器。

Result: 最大输出600 N·mm/A；10 kHz PWM下迟滞最小，非线性模型准确表示响应；相比PID，方波超调、下冲和稳态RMSE分别降低77.4%、61.9%和68.3%；成功跟踪正弦和生物力学参考，1.5小时测试仅温升2.5°C且无跟踪损失。

Conclusion: 该高保真扭矩渲染系统能显著提升人机协作的安全性、效率和直观性，为MRF触觉执行器的实际应用提供了完整解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate and stable torque rendering is essential for safe and perceptive human--machine interaction. Magnetorheological fluid (MRF)-based actuators offer a compact and rapidly controllable solution for haptic feedback, but their practical implementation requires reliable fluid sealing, low-hysteresis excitation, accurate torque control, and stable long-duration operation. This article presents an integrated MRF haptic system featuring a compact magnetically self-sealed rotary actuator, low-hysteresis PWM operation, high-fidelity model-based torque rendering, and stable performance during long-time operation. Magnetostatic simulation guides the arrangement of magnetic and nonmagnetic materials to focus flux in the multidisk torque and permanent-magnet sealing regions, enabling a maximum 600 N$\cdot$mm/A output. Experiments show that higher PWM frequencies reduce hysteresis and improve repeatability. At 10 kHz, the response is represented by a nonlinear model that varies with the direction and speed of torque change. The real-time controller combines feedforward, hysteresis compensation, PI feedback, and sliding-mode correction. Compared with PID, it reduces square-wave overshoot, undershoot, and steady-state RMSE by 77.4\%, 61.9\%, and 68.3\%, respectively. It tracks sinusoidal and biomechanics-model-based references, and a 1.5-h test shows only a 2.5 $^\circ$C rise near the coil with no clear tracking loss. This high-fidelity torque rendering will fundamentally transform human--robot collaboration by making interactions safer, more efficient, and more intuitive.

</details>


### [192] [World-Model-Grounded LLM Planning for AUV and ASV Navigation Near Offshore Wind Farms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19661)
*Markus Buchholz,Ignacio Carlucho,Yvan R. Petillot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合世界模型与大型语言模型（LLM）的机器人规划框架，通过物理感知的世界模型预测动作执行时长与效果，并采用轨迹优化和闭环重规划，显著提升海洋机器人在复杂环境中的任务成功率与避障能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽能将自然语言任务转化为机器人动作序列，但缺乏物理直觉，无法判断动作持续时间或是否会导致碰撞，限制了其在真实物理环境中的可靠性。

Method: 方法包括三个组件：物理约束的神经世界模型、三阶段梯度轨迹优化器，以及带置信域保护的模型预测控制（MPC）式闭环重规划器。语言模型决定任务目标，世界模型负责计算动作时长与可行性，并针对6自由度AUV和3自由度ASV进行适配。此外，针对ASV还提出了VLM辅助的语义映射流程，利用卫星图像、海图和天气预报API替代机载传感器。

Result: 在五个基准任务中，两类车辆均零碰撞到达目标。在GazeboSim仿真中，经过残差微调后，ASV的距目标误差比无物理基线降低70-82%，AUV降低约93%；同时世界模型预测误差（RMSE）分别降低69%（ASV）和60%（AUV）。ASV语义映射流程达到96%的可航行性准确率。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将世界模型与LLM规划器结合能有效弥补LLM的物理感知缺失，显著提升机器人在动态海洋环境中的安全性与任务效率，且VLM辅助映射可替代人工障碍物标注，增强了系统的实用性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models can turn a natural-language mission into a sequence of robot actions, but they do not have a sense of physics: they cannot judge how long a command should run, or whether it will make the robot drift into an obstacle. We proposed the use of a world model to expand the capabilities of Large Language model-based planners. Our method has three components: a physics-grounded neural world model, a three-phase gradient-based trajectory optimizer, and a Model Predictive Controller (MPC)-style closed-loop replanner with a trust-region guard. The language model decides what to do, and the world model decides how long, whether that means driving eight thrusters through 6 DOF or two differential thrusters through 3 DOF. We evaluate two marine vehicle classes operating near offshore wind infrastructure: a 6-DOF Autonomous Underwater Vehicle (AUV) and a 3-DOF differential-drive Autonomous Surface Vehicle (ASV). In five benchmark missions per platform, both vehicles reach every goal with zero predicted collisions, and both transfer to GazeboSim under ocean current, waves, and thruster dynamics, remaining collision-free and cutting GazeboSim goal-distance error versus the ungrounded baseline by 70-82% (ASV) and roughly 93% (AUV), after a residual fine-tuning pass that separately reduces surrogate rollout Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) by 60% (AUV) and 69% (ASV). For the ASV we further demonstrate a Vision language model (VLM)-assisted semantic-mapping pipeline that extracts obstacles and environmental context from satellite imagery, nautical charts, and forecast Application Programming Interface (API) instead of onboard sensors, reaching 96% navigability accuracy as a drop-in replacement for hand-specified obstacle geometry.

</details>


### [193] [Keeping the Franka Emika Panda alive: a ROS 2 stack with a reliable position interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19740)
*Antonio Langella,Davide Risi,Vincenzo Petrone,Enrico Ferrentino,Pasquale Chiacchio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个开源软件栈，恢复了Franka Emika Panda机器人的ROS 2支持，并解决了其外部位置控制接口长期不可靠的问题。通过分析振动和保护性停止的根源，引入了异步硬件接口、速率匹配机制和位置域参考生成策略，实验验证了其在运动规划、柔顺控制、位置控制操作和触觉遥操作中的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Franka Emika Panda机器人缺乏稳定、开放的ROS 2支持，其外部位置控制接口存在振动和保护性停止等不可靠问题，阻碍了安全、可复现的人机协作应用开发。

Method: 首先分析位置控制不稳定的根本原因（外部控制环时序和采样抖动），然后设计异步硬件接口解耦实时通信与ROS 2控制环，引入速率匹配机制处理慢速命令源，并提出位置域参考生成策略以生成平滑的位置命令。

Result: 实验证明该架构能可靠跟踪速度参考，减少官方实现引入的运动伪影，并在两个独立的Panda平台上通过了运动规划、柔顺控制、位置控制操作和触觉遥操作的验证。

Conclusion: 该工作为Panda机器人恢复了现代、可靠、开源的ROS 2生态系统，降低了开发安全、响应快速且可复现的人机协作应用的门槛。

Abstract: This paper presents an open-source software stack that restores ROS 2 support for the Franka Emika Panda robot while resolving the long-standing unreliability of its external position control interface. We first analyze the root causes of unstable position control and show that the observed vibrations and protective stops arise from the timing of the external control loop and sampling jitter, rather than from limitations of the robot itself. Building on this analysis, we introduce an asynchronous hardware interface that decouples real-time communication from the ROS 2 control loop, a rate-matching mechanism for slower command sources, and a position-domain reference generation strategy that produces reliable, smooth position commands. Experimental validation shows that the proposed architecture reliably tracks velocity references by reducing motion artifacts introduced by the official implementation, and the stack is validated across motion planning, compliance control, position-controlled manipulation, and haptic teleoperation on two independent Panda platforms. By restoring a modern, reliable, and open ROS 2 ecosystem for the Panda, this work lowers the barrier to developing safe, responsive, and reproducible human-robot collaboration applications that integrate planning, perception, interaction, and shared autonomy. Code and videos are available on our website at https://sites.google.com/view/fer-ros2/.

</details>


### [194] [SAGE: Ergodic Control for Autonomous and Adaptive Inspection of Subsea Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19671)
*Markus Buchholz,Ignacio Carlucho,Yvan R. Petillot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 SAGE，一种基于语义和自适应生成遍历性的遍历控制架构，用于水下采油树检测。它根据实时风险分布分配巡检时间，相比固定路线 A* 能更频繁地检查高风险阀门，并能在发现新泄漏时无需重新规划即可调整行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的固定路线巡检无法根据实时风险动态调整，导致高风险阀门可能被忽视，而低风险阀门被过度巡检，影响检测效率和及时性。

Method: 采用 SAGE（语义和自适应生成遍历性）控制架构，利用传感器数据实时计算风险分布，并通过遍历控制律分配机器人在不同位置的时间，使其与风险分布成正比。

Result: 在仿真中，对于高风险阀门，SAGE 的重新访问间隔为 5.8 秒，而固定 A* 为 8.1 秒（对所有阀门固定），使得泄漏被发现的时间缩短至原来约三分之二；且新发现泄漏后，SAGE 能自动调整行为，无需重新规划。

Conclusion: SAGE 在动态风险环境下优于固定路线，能更高效地分配巡检时间，并在无需人工干预的情况下自适应风险变化，提高了检测的实时性和安全性。

Abstract: Subsea Christmas Trees (XTs) are underwater structures that use valves for directing oil flow, needing constant inspection. But not every valve carries the same risk at the same time: a valve with a suspected leak needs to be revisited far more often than one with a clean history, and that risk picture changes during the mission as new leaks are found. To handle this, we present SAGE (Semantic and Adaptive Generative Ergodicity), an ergodic-control architecture that allocates vehicle time in proportion to a live, sensor-derived risk distribution rather than a scripted route. We study a two-XT scenario, with five valves in total, and compare a fixed-loop A* tour against SAGE. Both methods can be tuned to spend similar total time near a high-risk valve, but only ergodic control also checks it more often: in simulation, a dominant-risk valve was revisited every 5.8 s under ergodic control against a fixed 8.1 s for every valve under A*, regardless of risk, so a leak can go unnoticed for barely two-thirds as long. Because the tracked distribution is recomputed rather than planned once, a newly detected leak shifts vehicle behavior on the next control cycle with no explicit re-planning step and no operator in the loop, which a fixed tour cannot do without a discrete re-route. We derive the ergodic control law behind this behavior and report simulation results on the five-valve scenario.

</details>


### [195] [GOAG: Generative and Object-Agnostic Grasp Planner for Dexterous Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19759)
*Julien Merand,Boris Meden,Mathieu Grossard,Liming Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为GOAG的生成式、与对象无关的灵巧抓取规划器，通过学习抓手的接触表面分布，在推理时结合对象特征采样有效抓取配置，无需对象特定的训练数据，并在MultiDex数据集上达到86.93%的平均成功率，同时具有更快的处理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的多指抓取规划器因训练数据局限于特定对象而泛化能力差，需要一种不依赖对象特定数据的通用方法。

Method: 通过深度生成模型学习特定抓手的接触表面分布的紧凑潜在表征，仅在推理时引入对象特征，以采样与抓手兼容的有效接触区域。

Result: 在MultiDex数据集上平均成功率86.93%，生成大量抓取时处理速度显著更快，性能与特定训练的方法相当，且无需对象特定数据。

Conclusion: GOAG有效解决了传统数据驱动抓取规划器的泛化问题，验证了对象无关学习的优势，适用于多种抓手的仿真和真实场景。

Abstract: Multifingered grasping is a crucial robotic skill, but current deep-learning grasp planners often struggle to generalize to new objects because they are trained on limited, object-specific datasets. We introduce a fundamentally different approach, grounded in the observation that the gripper and the object share identical surface geometry at their mutual contact points. We propose GOAG: Generative and Object-Agnostic Grasp Planner for Dexterous Robotic Manipulation, a novel deep generative model that learns a compact latent representation of a specific gripper's contact surface distribution, enabling the efficient sampling of valid grasp configurations without relying on object-specific training data. We show that by introducing object features only at inference time, our model can effectively retrieve admissible contact areas that are compatible with the gripper's capabilities. We validate our approach through extensive experiments on established grasp protocols in both simulated and real-world scenarios, demonstrating its effectiveness with different grippers from the literature. Our method delivers state-of-the-art results on the objects from the MultiDex dataset, achieving an average success rate of 86.93%. It offers significantly faster processing when generating numerous grasps, while matching the performance of leading approaches specifically trained on this dataset. Unlike these methods, our approach does not rely on object-specific training data, highlighting the advantages of object-agnostic learning. It effectively addresses the generalization challenges faced by traditional data-driven grasp planners. Code and videos are available on our project website https://cea-list.github.io/goagweb/ .

</details>


### [196] [CoToGrasp: Contact-Topology-Conditioned Dexterous Grasp Synthesis via Canonical Workspace Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19776)
*Julien Merand,Boris Meden,Liming Chen,Mathieu Grossard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoToGrasp，一种基于接触拓扑条件生成多样化稳定抓取姿态的生成框架，通过特征规范化工作空间实现物体无关训练和零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧抓取规划器主要优化物理稳定性，忽略下游功能任务；基于人类抓取分类法的条件生成需昂贵的有标注数据集。

Method: 设计物体无关的生成框架，引入特征规范化工作空间将局部物体特征映射至抓取器中心域，学习固有接触流形，实现零样本泛化。

Result: 在DexGraspNet数据集上达到SOTA，优于现有分类法引导规划器，并在物理机器人平台验证可行性和运动学可行性。

Conclusion: CoToGrasp有效解决数据瓶颈，实现接触拓扑约束下的多样化稳定抓取合成，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Current dexterous grasp planners primarily optimize for physical stability, focusing on whether an object can be grasped rather than how it should be grasped to support downstream functional tasks. However, conditioning grasp synthesis on specific human grasp taxonomies typically requires prohibitively expensive, object-annotated datasets. To address these limitations, we propose CoToGrasp, a novel generative framework that synthesizes diverse, stable grasps strictly conditioned on specific contact topologies. To bypass the data collection bottleneck, CoToGrasp is trained entirely in an object-agnostic manner. We introduce a feature-based canonical workspace that projects local object features into a unified gripper-centric domain, effectively decoupling the semantic functional intent from the arbitrary object geometry. By learning the intrinsic contact manifold of the gripper within this workspace, our model achieves zero-shot generalization to unseen objects at inference. Extensive evaluations on the large-scale DexGraspNet dataset demonstrate that CoToGrasp achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming existing taxonomy-guided planners. Finally, we demonstrate the physical viability and kinematic feasibility of our synthesized contact topologies on a physical robot platform. Code is available on our project website https://cea-list.github.io/cotograspweb/ .

</details>


### [197] [MILD: Tractable Terrain Modeling for Learning Improved Bipedal Locomotion on Deformable Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19955)
*Zeren Luo,Jiahui Zhang,Zhe Xu,Wanyue Li,Xinqi Li,Xuechao Chen,Zhangguo Yu,Annan Tang,Peng Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MILD框架，结合物理接地离散元接触求解器和深度强化学习控制器，使双足机器人在可变地形上实现自然适应行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模拟器无法捕捉可变形地面的时空异质性，限制了双足机器人在实际柔软地形上的移动能力。

Method: 使用物理接地离散元接触模型模拟空间变化的足地交互，并通过带潜在调制和本体感觉估计的深度强化学习训练地形感知控制器。

Result: 与先进方法相比，训练中生成更多样真实的接触场景，控制器在真实柔软表面表现出自然适应，硬件实验验证了在线地形识别与适应能力。

Conclusion: MILD有效提升双足机器人在可变形地形上的适应性与鲁棒性，为实际应用提供可行方案。

Abstract: Enabling robots to walk on yielding terrain is vital for applications ranging from disaster response to planetary exploration. While bipedal robots hold immense potential, their locomotion on deformable surfaces remains limited as current simulators fail to capture the spatiotemporal heterogeneity of such yielding substrates. We present MILD, featuring a physics-grounded discrete-element contact solver that accurately simulates spatially varying foot-terrain interactions. Complementing this model, we train a terrain-aware locomotion controller via deep reinforcement learning with latent modulation and proprioceptive estimation. Quantitative comparisons against state-of-the-art methods show our approach generates more diverse and realistic contact scenarios during training, resulting in controllers that exhibit natural adaptation on real deformable surfaces. Through hardware experiments, we demonstrate the system's capability for online terrain identification and adaptation across a wide range of surface stiffness.

</details>


### [198] [PVRA: A Pointwise Key-point Voting Framework for Robotic Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19968)
*Kulunu Samarawickrama,Roel Pieters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于3D关键点的模块化学习框架，用于学习装配依赖关系，从而在RGB-D输入下推断可操作的输出，实现自主装配操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机视觉实现部分自主装配，但自主装配需要额外技能，尤其是学习装配依赖关系以预测有意义的行为输出。

Method: 通过比较分析相关领域研究，提出3D关键点模块化学习框架，训练网络学习装配依赖关系，并在装配姿态估计数据集上评估。

Result: 该框架在装配姿态估计数据集上优于以物体为中心的基线方法，使用了针对渐进装配的增强指标。

Conclusion: 自主装配操纵需要从物体感知向学习装配依赖关系的进步，所提出的框架能有效推断可操作输出。

Abstract: Modern computer vision has enabled partial autonomy in robotic assembly manipulation. However, performing autonomous manipulation of a progressive assembly demands a more specific set of skills, in addition to perceiving the objects. Through a comparative analysis of research in the associated domains, we deduce that object-centric perception must advance towards learning assembly dependencies to predict meaningful actionable outputs for autonomous assembly manipulation. Subsequently, we present a 3D keypoint-based modular learning framework to learn assembly dependencies to infer actionable outputs given a RGB-D input of an assembly scene. We train and evaluate our trained network on an assembly pose estimation dataset and compare it against object-centric baselines with an augmented set of metrics for progressive assemblies.

</details>


### [199] [Learning Highly Dynamic Skills Transition for Quadruped Jumping Through Constrained Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19977)
*Zeren Luo,Jiahui Zhang,Yimin Han,Ji Ma,Minghao Lu,Ioannis Havoutis,Peng Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层强化学习流程，使四足机器人能够模仿动物动作，自主识别并动态穿越狭窄门等受限障碍，并扩展至其他高动态任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然腿足动物能在受限空间中执行爆发性动作，但在四足机器人上复现这一行为一直是长期挑战，现有方法难以实现自主、敏捷的穿越狭窄障碍。

Method: 采用分层强化学习：低层策略通过模仿学习训练，模仿真实动物行为形成多种技能；高层控制器利用视觉检测获取门信息，并结合低层能力规划无碰撞轨迹以选择合适动作。

Result: 成功实现了四足机器人自主、敏捷的空中穿越狭窄门任务，并验证了框架可扩展至其他高动态任务，是地面行走机器人完成此类任务的首批工作之一。

Conclusion: 该分层强化学习框架有效提升了四足机器人的敏捷性和自主性，使其更接近生物的真实运动能力，并为未来扩展至更复杂场景提供了可能。

Abstract: Although legged animals are capable of performing explosive motions while traversing confined spaces, replicating this behavior in quadrupedal robots has been a longstanding challenge. Here, we propose a hierarchical reinforcement learning pipeline that empowers the robots to perform aggressive locomotion through constrained obstacles--a narrow gate. The imitation learning technique is used to train the low-level policy, which mimics the behaviors of real animals and forms a set of diverse skills. The high-level controller, having an awareness of the capability of low-level skills and acquiring the gate information via vision-based detection, determines the suitable maneuvers with collision-free trajectories to traverse it dynamically. Notably, we also verify that this framework can be extended to other highly dynamic tasks. This is one of the first works that perform autonomous and agile aerial gate traversal tasks on ground-walking robots, extending the lifelike agility of legged robots to match that of their biological counterparts.

</details>


### [200] [Evidence-Gated Task and Motion Planning with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20084)
*Tsunehiko Tanaka,Matthew Stephenson,Alistair Macvicar,Edgar Simo-Serra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出证据获取与可行性门控框架，结合VLM和TAMP，通过探索子目标获取视觉证据，并利用可行性门控决定继续规划、进一步探索或停止，以提升长时程操作任务在部分可观测环境下的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测环境下，机器人执行长时程自然语言操作任务时，结合VLM和TAMP的方法可能依赖VLM先验知识生成子目标，缺乏观测支持，导致执行失败或意外结果。

Method: EAFG框架：由VLM生成探索性子目标，经TAMP执行获取视觉证据，然后通过可行性门控判断是否继续任务规划、获取更多证据或停止。

Result: 在烹饪任务中，当物体使用模糊时，EAFG通过提前发现任务相关物体提高了食谱完成率；对于需要缺失物体的指令，EAFG促进了合理的停止决策，并减少了重复尝试操作该物体的次数。

Conclusion: EAFG通过显式获取证据和可行性门控，有效提升了部分可观测环境下长时程操作任务的鲁棒性和安全性。

Abstract: Robots executing long-horizon manipulation tasks from natural-language instructions must reason about both semantic task structure and geometric feasibility. However, under partial observability, the availability of goal-relevant objects may be uncertain. In such cases, approaches that combine Vision-Language Models (VLMs) with Task and Motion Planning (TAMP) may generate subgoals that rely on the VLM's prior knowledge without observational support, leading to execution failures or unintended outcomes. We propose Evidence Acquisition and Feasibility Gating (EAFG), a framework that acquires visual evidence through VLM-generated exploratory subgoals and TAMP-based execution. EAFG then applies a feasibility gate to decide whether to proceed with task planning, acquire further evidence, or halt. Our experiments show that, in cooking tasks with ambiguous object use, EAFG improves recipe completion by discovering task-relevant objects before planning. For instructions requiring an absent object, EAFG promotes appropriate halt decisions and reduces repeated attempts to manipulate that object.

</details>


### [201] [Towards Professional Tennis Styles for Humanoid Robots with Adaptive Motion Planning and Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20087)
*Tao Huang,Ruofei Liu,Xuchen Tang,Xinyin Zhang,Junli Ren,Huayi Wang,Feiyu Jia,Yukai Qi,Kangning Yin,Weishuai Zeng,Lipeng Chen,Xi Li,Ting Wu,Kailin Li,Ruoli Dai,Jingbo Wang,Lei Han,Jiangmiao Pang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaPT框架，通过分层设计与自适应机制，从广播视频学习专业网球动作，并成功迁移到真实人形机器人，实现野外发球。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在球类运动中虽展现潜力，但难以兼顾专业动作风格与任务性能，且仿真到现实的迁移存在跟踪性能下降、规划误差累积和感知噪声等问题。

Method: 采用分层设计：规划器生成风格化运动，跟踪器执行动作；通过自适应机制学习跟踪随机化执行速度，并在规划器中引入运动速度适配器以减轻误差累积。

Result: 在Unitree G1上验证了自适应机制能有效缩小仿真到现实的差距；并在全尺寸Dobot Atom人形机器人上实现了无需动作捕捉的野外发球。

Conclusion: AdaPT成功实现了从视频学习到真实机器人部署的端到端流程，并提供了未来人形机器人球类系统设计的算法与工程见解。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have recently demonstrated promising capabilities in real-world ball sports. However, achieving professional motion styles while maintaining strong task performance remains challenging. In this work, we propose AdaPT, an Adaptive Motion Planning and Tracking framework that learns professional tennis serving and rally styles directly from broadcast videos. This hierarchical design is motivated by the key insight that the planner generates stylistic kinematic motions, while the tracker executes them with minimal interference with planning. Despite its effectiveness in simulation, a substantial sim-to-real gap emerges: tracking performance inevitably degrades on real robots, and this degradation is partially overlooked by autoregressive planning and further compounded by noisy perception. To address these issues, our adaptation mechanism improves tracking robustness by learning to track randomized execution speeds, while conditioning the planner on a learned motion-speed adapter to mitigate compounding errors. Real-world experiments on the Unitree G1 demonstrate the effectiveness of our adaptation mechanism in bridging the sim-to-real gap. We further deploy AdaPT policies on the full-size Dobot Atom humanoid robot (1.7m) and demonstrate in-the-wild serving without motion capture. Beyond these results, our real-world experiments reveal both algorithmic and engineering insights for future humanoid ball-sports systems. Videos and code are available on our \href{https://humanoidtennis.github.io/AdaPT/}{project website}.

</details>


### [202] [Planning-Oriented End-to-End Autonomous Driving: Architectures, Evaluation, and Emerging Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20111)
*Yanchen Guan,Xingcheng Liu,Bin Rao,Chengyue Wang,Guofa Li,Yunjian Li,Lishengsa Yue,Zhiyong Cui,Chengzhong Xu,Zhenning Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了端到端自动驾驶从相机到控制的回归方法向规划导向系统的演变，提出了一个四轴分类框架（输入表示、规划输出、监督信号、评估协议），并强调现代端到端驾驶的关键在于中间表示是否被学习、监督和评估以支持安全、可行和符合路线的规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶研究快速发展，但缺乏对从行为克隆到视觉-语言-动作系统等方法的系统梳理，且现有评估指标（如开环轨迹匹配）不足以证明安全和人机对齐的驾驶性能。

Method: 通过文献调研，构建了四轴（输入表示、规划输出、监督信号、评估协议）来组织现有方法，并分析了从开环到闭环评估、从单指标到人类偏好感知指标的基准转变。

Result: 指出架构进步难以在没有基准一致评估的情况下解释，位移式开环指标不能充分反映安全和人机对齐；总结了不确定性感知规划、学习者-专家不匹配、运行时安全保证、语言-动作接地、世界模型验证和可复现基准等开放挑战。

Conclusion: 端到端驾驶的核心不是是否使用中间表示，而是它们如何被学习、监督和评估以支撑安全规划；未来需要更一致、更现实的评估协议和解决多种开放挑战。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving has evolved from camera-to-control regression toward planning-oriented systems that use structured representations, trajectory-level outputs, and increasingly realistic evaluation protocols. This survey reviews this transition across behavior cloning, conditional imitation learning, privileged distillation, BEV and vectorized planning, unified perception-prediction-planning architectures, world-model-based planners, and vision-language-action systems. We argue that the key distinction in modern end-to-end driving is not whether intermediate representations are used, but whether they are learned, supervised, and evaluated to support safe, feasible, and route-compliant planning. To organize the literature, we synthesize existing methods along four axes: input representation, planning output, supervision signal, and evaluation protocol. We further examine the benchmark shift from open-loop trajectory matching to closed-loop simulation, non-reactive real-log evaluation, long-tail testing, and human-preference-aware metrics. Our analysis highlights that architectural progress is difficult to interpret without benchmark-consistent evaluation, and that displacement-based open-loop metrics alone provide limited evidence for safe and human-aligned driving. We conclude with open challenges in uncertainty-aware planning, learner-expert mismatch, runtime safety assurance, language-action grounding, world-model validation, and reproducible benchmarking.

</details>


### [203] [DART-S: Reachability-Audited Active-Suspension Preconditioning for Off-Road Vehicle Jumps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20275)
*Yu Hu,Fangzhou Zhao,Liang Chen,Chen Min,Wei Li,Mingyuan Sang,Jiajia Ma,Shican Chen,Di Pang,Baolei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DART-S 通过坡道悬挂预调节改变起飞前的俯仰状态与轮速，利用局部校准映射和区间可达性筛选，显著提升姿态恢复成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中扭矩反应无法恢复超出轮角动量预算的起飞误差，需要提前调整状态以扩展可用控制权限。

Method: 坡道悬挂预调节、局部校准映射、支持性选择器、区间可达性筛选、精确配对审计。

Result: 在600次运行中所有查询分支正确；边界条件下DART-S成功率24/24 vs DART 0/24；定时动作成功率23/24 vs 静态预设0/24，且无驱动系统超限。

Conclusion: DART-S通过预调节有效扩大控制权限，显著提升起飞后姿态恢复能力，且具有安全性保障。

Abstract: Airborne torque reaction cannot recover takeoff errors beyond the wheel angular-momentum budget. DART-S applies ramp-face suspension preconditioning to change pitch, pitch rate, and wheel spin before liftoff, thereby shifting the queried state and altering the remaining authority budget. To predict how each suspension action reshapes this state-budget pair, DART-S employs a local calibration map. A support-aware selector combines the predicted shift with local outcome evidence and an interval-reachability screen; an exact-pair audit reports residual authority. Across 600 new runs in 72 independent BeamNG sessions, every positive, negative, and boundary query follows its prespecified branch. At the confirmed 40°/13 m/s boundary, DART-S attains 24/24 post-touchdown attitude-criterion successes versus 0/24 for DART (session-level Holm-adjusted p=0.0234). At 11.5 m/s, a 0.35 s timing action attains 23/24 versus 0/24 for the static preset (p=0.0156). The 200 rad/s command guard keeps drivetrain hard-limit exceedance at zero across all 600 runs. The source code will be available at https://github.com/MeridianCAS/DART-S

</details>


### [204] [Video2DoorTraversal: Push Door Traversal via Simulated Door Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20251)
*Xincheng Tang,Yiji Chen,Youhan Xie,Wanyu Li,Zhengjie Shu,Lai Jiang,Wenkang Hu,Yitong Li,Jinchuang Zhang,Xibin Song,Ruigang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Video2DoorTraversal，一种基于单视频的 real-to-sim-to-real 框架，使轮腿移动操作机器人能够打开并穿越未知门。该框架通过 DoorTwin 重建实例对齐的门孪生体，利用仿真代理生成演示，并训练 ArticuACT 双深度策略，在真实五扇门上平均成功率达 96.57%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 门开启与穿越是长时域腿臂操作任务，需要精细的把手交互和身体-手臂协调控制。现有方法依赖多视图重建或手工设计技能，缺乏从单视角视频直接迁移到现实机器人的能力。

Method: 提出 Video2DoorTraversal，包含：1) DoorTwin：从单 RGB 视频重建实例对齐的、带关节的门孪生体；2) 仿真代理将关节信息转换为参数化技能程序，并迭代优化失败轨迹以生成可执行演示；3) ArticuACT：一种双深度感知策略，基于机器人中心摄像机和交互感知监督，预测协调的基座、手臂和夹爪指令。

Result: 在五扇真实门上的平均成功率为 96.57%，对结构相似的未见门零样本成功率为 80.95%，完整任务（接近、开门、穿越）平均耗时约 13 秒。

Conclusion: 该框架验证了单视频仿真的可行性，实现了高效的零样本门操作迁移，为复杂腿臂操作任务提供了低成本解决方案。

Abstract: Door opening and traversal is a long-horizon loco-manipulation task that requires precise handle interaction and coordinated base-arm control. We present Video2DoorTraversal, a single-video real-to-sim-to-real framework for wheel-legged mobile manipulators. Given one RGB video of a real door, DoorTwin reconstructs an instance-aligned, articulated, and simulation-ready door twin with realistic geometry and appearance. A simulation-in-the-loop agent converts the recovered articulation into a parameterized skill program and iteratively refines failed rollouts to generate physically executable demonstrations. These demonstrations are used to train ArticuACT, a dual-depth policy that predicts coordinated base, arm, and gripper commands using robot-centric camera conditioning and interaction-aware supervision. With all perception and policy inference running onboard, the system achieves a 96.57% average success rate across five real doors and an 80.95% zero-shot success rate on structurally similar unseen doors, while completing the full approach, opening, and traversal sequence in approximately 13s on average. Project Page: https://video2doortraversal.github.io/.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [An end-to-end differentiable transient vapor-compression framework for automated machine sizing and unified optimal control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19552)
*Sam Yang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于JAX的端到端可微分有限体积蒸气压缩框架，统一了设备尺寸设计、动态仿真和模型预测控制，无需参数拟合即可准确预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热泵电气化需要动态、电网响应式运行，但现有工程方法在静态选型、瞬态仿真和最优控制之间割裂，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 利用JAX实现有限体积框架，通过预计算的(p,h)双线性流形和解析自动微分避免迭代求解，并采用L-稳定TR-BDF2积分和隐式欧拉MPC共享同一物理内核。

Result: 在16个迷你分体运行场景中，制冷能力预测MAPE为7.37%，在公用事业规模硬件在环测试中将周期内制冷误差控制在1.19%–1.62%以内。

Conclusion: 该框架为自动化机器合成、动态电网协调和梯度驱动的硬件-控制协同设计提供了开源且可微的基础。

Abstract: Accelerating the electrification of thermal energy requires vapor-compression heat pumps capable of dynamic, grid-responsive operation. However, equipment engineering remains fragmented across static rating-point selection, stiff multi-phase transient simulation, and gradient-based optimal control. Here, we present an end-to-end differentiable, finite-volume vapor-compression framework implemented natively in JAX that automates machine sizing directly from stated thermal duties and unifies dynamic simulation with predictive control under a single compiled residual $\dot{y}={f}(t,{y},{u})$. Thermodynamic evaluations bypass runtime root-finding via bilinear $(p,h)$ manifolds pre-flashed from Helmholtz equations of state, enabling analytical forward-mode automatic differentiation. Mass conservation across multi-phase coils is strictly preserved by incorporating both $(\partialρ/\partial p)_h$ and $(\partialρ/\partial h)_p$ partial derivatives into the dynamic pressure differential equation. The sizer directly inverts compressor displacement, electronic expansion valve area, and heat-exchanger tube counts via four-point cycle synthesis and $\varepsilon$-NTU matching using the identical polytropic compressor map. Crucially, the compiled physics kernel is shared symmetrically between $L$-stable TR-BDF2 stiff integration and implicit-Euler Model Predictive Control (MPC), eliminating plant-controller surrogate mismatch. Validated against open-access experimental benchmarks without parameter fitting, the framework predicts cooling capacity with $7.37\%$ MAPE across 16 mini-split operational runs and bounds on-period cooling error within $1.19\%$--$1.62\%$ on utility-scale Hardware-in-the-Loop traces. This work provides an open-source, differentiable foundation for automated machine synthesis, dynamic grid orchestration, and gradient-based hardware-control co-design.

</details>


### [206] [The Calibration Illusion in Traffic Microsimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19642)
*Cameron Hickert,Maryam Samaei,Athena Wang,Chengyuan Zhang,Lijun Sun,Yanbing Wang,Mostafa Ameli,Cathy Wu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文指出公路交通微观仿真的“自动”标定在很大程度上是假象，因为大量定制化手动工作隐藏在算法背后，阻碍了客观比较和科学进展。为此，作者提出了一个综合基准测试，以揭示这一假象并提供统一的评估标准，实验结果展示了算法无需定制调优时的基线表现，并分析了大规模标定误差的来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手动标定耗时且主观，而自动标定方法虽日益流行，但其中隐藏的大量人工定制工作未被量化，导致方法间难以进行客观比较，影响可复现性和研究累积。

Method: 提出了一个综合基准测试框架，涵盖多种场景，用于评估公路微观仿真标定算法的性能，无需针对特定场景定制调优。

Result: 基准测试展示了算法在无定制调优下的基线结果，揭示了研究空白，并发现大规模公路标定中的误差来源与简化设置下的开发条件不同。

Conclusion: 自动标定并非完全自动，需要统一的基准来促进客观比较和累积科学进步。该基准为评估标定算法提供了公共标尺，并为未来研究指明方向。

Abstract: The transportation community seeks to use calibration methods for highway traffic microsimulation. This is a response to the time-consuming and subjective nature of traditional manual calibration, as well as the growing prevalence of data for calibration. This work argues that this "automatic" calibration is largely an illusion. A significant - and unquantified - amount of bespoke manual work is hidden behind these methods. This illusion inhibits a core component of scientific advancement: objective comparison against a shared standard. This impedes evaluation, undermines reproducibility, and fragments research. To address this gap, this paper introduces a comprehensive benchmark designed to simultaneously expose the calibration illusion for highway microsimulation and provide a common ruler. The results across a range of scenarios present a new baseline for what the algorithms can achieve without bespoke tuning, revealing the research gap that remains and providing a tool to advance a cumulative science of calibration. Additional experiments provide insights into the source of calibration errors that arise in large-scale highway calibration relative to the simplified settings under which methods are commonly developed.

</details>


### [207] [Reachability-based Time-domain Distance Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19678)
*Joshua A. Taylor,Nathan Baeckeland,Alejandro D. Domínguez-García*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文通过可达集和基于集合的状态估计来表征距离继电器在故障期间可观测的时域测量，并构建降阶二维模型以高效定义瞬时故障测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 距离继电器对输电线路故障的检测依赖本地测量，但现有方法可能未能充分捕捉测量集的边界，且一般可达集计算不可解，需要高效且与标准距离保护一致的模型。

Method: 将网络建模为RLC电路，利用可达集和集合状态估计定义故障测试；针对每种故障类型构建降阶二维模型，基于继电器视在电压和电流，将故障测试转化为未求解的微分方程。

Result: 所提方法使计算高效，并与距离保护标准量一致，能够定义瞬时故障测试。

Conclusion: 该方法为距离继电器故障检测提供了新的理论框架，通过降阶模型实现了高效且实用的测试定义。

Abstract: Distance relays detect faults on transmission lines from local voltage and current measurements. In this paper, we characterize the set of time-domain measurements a distance relay can observe during a fault as a reachable set. We define corresponding fault tests in terms of set-based state estimation. This allows us to model the full network as an RLC circuit with voltage and current sources. In general, computing reachable sets and set-based state estimates is intractable. For each fault type, we construct a reduced-order, two-dimensional model in terms of the apparent voltage and current seen by the relay. This makes all computations efficient, aligns with standard quantities in distance protection, and allows us to define instantaneous fault tests in terms of unsolved differential equations.

</details>


### [208] [A simulation based dataset of faults and events for machine learning in power systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19777)
*Georg Kordowich,Jonathan Loebel,Julian Oelhaf,Andreas Maier,Siming Bayer,Christian Bergler,Johann Jaeger*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了EvEMTBench，一个基于电磁暂态仿真的合成数据集，用于训练、微调和基准测试机器学习模型，以解决逆变器型可再生能源并网带来的电力系统保护挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆变器型可再生能源并网给传统电力系统保护带来挑战，机器学习方法可应对，但缺乏开放数据集阻碍了可重复性和公平比较。

Method: 通过电磁暂态仿真生成合成数据集，验证仿真参数确保物理合理性，提供详细文档，包含多种拓扑和电压等级下9600 Hz的同步点电压电流测量。

Result: 数据集覆盖多种故障和运行事件，支持早期故障检测、故障定位和事件检测等任务。

Conclusion: EvEMTBench为机器学习在电力系统保护中的应用提供了标准化基准，促进了可重复研究和算法比较。

Abstract: The integration of inverter-based renewable energy sources into electric grids challenges conventional power system protection. Machine learning-based solutions can address these challenges by utilizing available data in modern smart grids. However, the lack of open datasets prevents reproducibility and fair comparisons between different approaches and their results, which hinders further progress. Therefore, this paper presents EvEMTBench, a synthetic dataset of faults and events generated using electromagnetic transient simulations. The physical plausibility of the power system simulation is ensured by validating the simulation parameters against established literature and providing comprehensive documentation of the simulation procedure. The dataset is designed for training, fine-tuning, and benchmarking machine learning models by providing synchronized point-on-wave voltage and current measurements at 9600 Hz across a diverse set of topologies and voltage levels. The inclusion of a wide range of fault and operating events allows the utilization of EvEMTBench for different tasks like incipient fault detection, fault localization, or event detection.

</details>


### [209] [Large reasoning models for abnormal situation management in safety-critical industrial processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19819)
*Khalid Alhazmi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种通用大型推理模型，无需任务特定训练，仅利用操作员可获得的信息，通过程序化验证的受限动作接口，在运行时成功管理工业过程的异常情况。在39种异常情况和操作点变化中，模型在所有情况下均维持了硬约束，而基础调节控制失败15次；模型匹配专家设计的先进控制，并在15个安全关键场景中全部正确诊断根因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化系统仅在设计包络内运行安全关键过程，异常情况留给操作员处理，但操作员管理不当是过程安全事故的主要原因，阻碍了自主性的实现。因此，需要一种无需人工干预的运行时异常管理方法。

Method: 使用通用大型推理模型，不进行任务特定训练，仅利用操作员可获得的信息，通过一个有界且程序化验证的动作接口在运行时管理异常情况。

Result: 在39个异常情况和操作点变化中，模型在所有情况下维持了所有硬约束，而基本调节控制失败15次；模型匹配专家设计的先进控制，并在15个安全关键场景中全部正确诊断根因。三个独立开发的模型（成本跨度达30倍）均超过基线。

Conclusion: 在完全可审计的评估中，这些结果表明无需人工参与即可实现运行时异常情况管理，展示了大型推理模型在工业过程安全中的潜力。

Abstract: Automation operates safety-critical processes inside their design envelope and leaves abnormal situations to human operators. Mismanagement of these situations is a leading contributor to process-safety incidents and a hindrance to achieving autonomy. Here we show that a general-purpose large reasoning model, with no task-specific training and only the information available to an operator, manages abnormal situations at run time through a bounded, programmatically verified action interface. Across 39 abnormal situations and operating-point changes on a plant-wide industrial benchmark process, the reasoning model maintained the plant within all hard constraints in all 39, while basic regulatory control failed in 15. It matched the plant's expert-engineered advanced control and diagnosed the root-cause fault in 15 of 15 safety-critical situations. Three independently developed models spanning a thirty-fold cost range exceeded the baseline. In a fully auditable evaluation, these results demonstrate run-time abnormal situation management without a human in the loop.

</details>


### [210] [Fixed-structure Gaussian Mixture Filtering with Robust Measurement Updates under Outliers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19895)
*Ondřej Straka,Uwe D. Hanebeck*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于固定结构高斯混合滤波的鲁棒测量更新方法，通过离线分解转移密度和变分贝叶斯处理学生t分布离群值，用于离散时间非线性随机系统的状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在测量离群值的非线性系统中，标准高斯混合滤波性能下降，需要鲁棒的测量更新策略。

Method: 采用固定结构高斯混合框架，离线将转移密度分解为轴对齐高斯分量；测量异常用学生t分布建模，并通过变分贝叶斯近似更新每个高斯分量。

Result: 在三维跟踪场景中，带离群值的距离和方位测量下，所提滤波器有效处理了重尾噪声。

Conclusion: 该方法通过确定性可调的高斯混合结构和变分贝叶斯更新，提升了离群值存在时的状态估计鲁棒性。

Abstract: Bayesian state estimation for discrete-time nonlinear stochastic systems is considered in the presence of measurement outliers. Building on a fixed-structure Gaussian mixture filtering framework, this paper proposes a robust measurement-update variant in which the predictive density structure is determined by an offline decomposition of the transition density into axis-aligned Gaussian components. This construction maintains the Gaussian mixture structure as deterministic and tunable via the chosen decomposition fidelity. Measurement components affected by outliers are modeled using a Student's-t distribution, and the corresponding update of each Gaussian mixture component is approximated by a variational Bayes procedure. The resulting filter is evaluated in a three-dimensional tracking scenario with range and bearing measurements, where the bearing channel is affected by outliers, modeled as heavy-tailed noise.

</details>


### [211] [Harmonic Stability of Power Systems: A Control-Theoretic Definition and Assessment Criteria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19975)
*N. Bhoir,A. Sarkar,J. E. Machado,J. Schiffer*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文首次形式化定义了谐波稳定性，结合BIBO与内部稳定性概念，并证明非线性换流器系统的局部谐波稳定性可由其线性时周期近似决定，同时提出基于谐波状态空间的LMI条件用于稳定性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谐波相互作用已成为换流器主导电力系统中的关键动态稳定性现象，但缺乏对谐波稳定性的严格数学定义与系统分析框架。

Method: 定义谐波稳定性为BIBO与内部稳定性的组合；理论证明非线性系统的谐波稳定性可由LTP近似保证；基于谐波状态空间（HSS）表示推导时不变LMI条件进行可计算评估。

Result: 在电网跟随型换流器系统中，HSS分析数值验证了周期运行轨迹附近的谐波稳定性。

Conclusion: 所提出的形式化定义与HSS-LMI框架为谐波稳定性提供了严格理论支撑和有效计算方法，适用于换流器主导电力系统分析。

Abstract: Harmonic interactions have become a defining dynamic stability phenomenon in converter-based power systems (CBPSs). However, a formalization of the notion of harmonic stability in the context of nonlinear dynamical systems is not available thus far. In this paper, we propose a definition of harmonic stability formulated as a combination of two standard stability notions, namely, bounded-input bounded-output (BIBO) and internal stability properties with respect to any nominal periodic trajectory. Moreover, we rigorously show that the local harmonic stability of any nominal periodic trajectory of nonlinear CBPSs is implied by the stability properties of its linear time-periodic (LTP) approximation. In addition, to enable computationally tractable stability assessment, we develop a framework based on harmonic state-space (HSS) representations of LTP models. In particular, we provide time-invariant linear matrix inequality (LMI) conditions to certify harmonic stability. The proposed methodology is illustrated on a grid-following converter system, where the HSS-based analysis numerically confirms harmonic stability around the periodic operating trajectory.

</details>


### [212] [Validation of a driver model for energy consumption simulations of road vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19980)
*Luigi Romano,Michele Godio,Fredrik Bruzelius,Pär Johannesson*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种与运行条件框架兼容的简单驾驶员模型，用于将驾驶环境转化为速度曲线，并通过驾驶模拟器和真实车辆数据进行验证，结果表明该模型能有效再现驾驶员行为的主要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统驾驶循环方法依赖预设速度曲线，而新兴的运行条件框架用道路、限速、交通和天气等因素描述驾驶环境，但缺少将运行条件转化为速度曲线的驾驶员模型。

Method: 提出一个简单的驾驶员模型，该模型与运行条件框架兼容，通过驾驶模拟器研究和真实车辆日志数据识别模型参数，并评估其预测性能。

Result: 模型能够有效再现驾驶员行为的主要影响，但全面评估其有效性仍需进一步研究。

Conclusion: 该简单驾驶员模型在运行条件框架下具有潜力，但需要更多研究以完善和验证其适用性。

Abstract: The energy performance of road vehicles has traditionally been evaluated using driving cycles, in which a prescribed speed profile is tracked either in simulation or by a physical vehicle. Recent research has proposed an alternative framework based on operating conditions, where the driving environment is described in terms of factors such as road topography, legal speed limits, traffic, and weather, rather than by an explicit speed profile. This paper addresses the complementary problem of driver modeling, necessary to translate the operating conditions to a speed profile. A simple driver model designed to be compatible with the operating-condition framework and evaluates its ability to reproduce realistic driving behavior is investigated. Validation of the model is done through a controlled driving-simulator study vehicle log files collected during real-world operation and are used to identify the model parameters and assess the model's predictive performance. The results suggest that this simple model can be efficient in reproducing major effects of driver behavior, while further research is required to fully assess its validity.

</details>


### [213] [Wave-Based Bilateral Teleoperation between Nonlinear Manipulators with Direct Contact Force Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20043)
*G. Q. Bao Tran,Takanori Miyoshi,Ho Duc Tho*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对非线性多自由度机械臂在恒定通信时延下的双边遥操作问题，提出了一种基于LMI的无源性短缺表征和上严格无源通信律，以实现力反馈下的稳定性和位置力同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典波变换架构传输协调力，而直接反馈环境力可能使闭环系统不稳定。为了提高遥操作透明度，需要在不牺牲稳定性的前提下引入接触力反馈。

Method: 通过LMI方法刻画Euler-Lagrange远端系统的无源性短缺，设计上严格无源通信律补偿该短缺，保证时延下闭环稳定性及位置和力同步。

Result: 在非线性2自由度机械臂的多种仿真设置中验证了所提方法在稳定性和同步方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够在通信时延下实现接触力反馈的遥操作，同时保持稳定性和同步性能，为高透明度遥操作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We study bilateral teleoperation between nonlinear, multi-DOF robotic manipulators in the presence of constant communication delays. Unlike classical wave-transformation architectures that transmit a coordinating force, we consider the case where the environmental force is reflected to the master side to enhance teleoperation transparency. Since direct contact force feedback might destabilize the closed-loop system, we first develop a passivity-shortage characterization for the Euler--Lagrange remote system using a linear matrix inequality (LMI) approach. An upper strictly passive communication law is then employed to compensate for the computed passivity shortage so that the closed-loop stability under delays as well as position and force synchronization are preserved under appropriate conditions. Simulations with nonlinear 2-DOF robotic manipulators in different settings illustrate our approach.

</details>


### [214] [Performance-Guaranteed Reference Tracking With Power Directionality Constraints: Application to Controlled Stochastic Watersheds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20120)
*Jonathan Shell,Sepehr Moalemi,Branko Kerkez,Jeff Scruggs*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对具有功率方向性约束的随机控制综合方法，可提供均方参考跟踪的性能保证，并将其应用于真实智慧水系统的洪水缓解示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代雨水基础设施面临容量需求增加，传统通过新建资产应对成本高昂，而反馈控制虽有效但引入功率方向性约束，导致反馈综合中的非线性约束。

Method: 扩展了性能保证控制（PGC）框架，从干扰抑制扩展到参考跟踪，并开发了随机控制综合程序，提供均方参考跟踪的性能界限。

Result: 在真实世界智慧水系统的数值模型上成功应用于洪水缓解，证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 关键成果是将PGC框架扩展至参考跟踪，为具有功率方向性约束的系统提供性能保证的控制综合方法。

Abstract: Modern stormwater infrastructure faces increased demands that require a corresponding increase in capacity. Traditionally, these demands have been met by constructing new infrastructure assets, which is a costly endeavor. More recently, many system operators have achieved great success in employing feedback control techniques to improve system performance. However, the resulting closed-loop system exhibits power directionality constraints that introduce nonlinear constraints in feedback synthesis. In this work, we develop a stochastic control synthesis procedure with provable performance bounds on mean-square reference tracking for a general class of problems in which power directionality constraints arise. The proposed method is then applied to a flood mitigation example using a numerical model of a real-world smart water system. The key result is an extension of the performance-guaranteed control (PGC) framework, which was originally designed for disturbance rejection, to accommodate reference tracking control objectives.

</details>


### [215] [Adaptive RBFNN Control of Uncertain Bilateral Teleoperation Systems with Delay-Dependent LMI Stability Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20182)
*Mohammadali Ghaemifar,Arshia Goshtasbi,Arian Hajizadeh,Armin Attarzadeh,Erfan Riazati*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种紧凑的双边自适应控制器，用于具有恒定前后延迟的非线性双边遥操作系统。每侧仅使用两个标量自适应参数，通过σ修正更新，并利用Lyapunov-Krasovskii泛函和自由权矩阵保证同步误差的一致最终有界性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于RBFNN的遥操作控制器中，自适应参数数量随网络规模增加，导致计算负担重，且不确定性分解复杂。本文旨在减少自适应参数数量，同时保持稳定性和同步性能。

Method: 将操作者和环境阻抗纳入机械臂动力学，每侧仅估计理想RBF权重范数和摩擦力、逼近误差及扰动的综合影响。采用σ修正更新两个标量参数。构建包含滑模变量能量、估计误差和延迟积分项的Lyapunov-Krasovskii泛函，基于滑模面恒等式引入自由权矩阵，推导延迟相关的矩阵条件。

Result: 在2自由度旋转机械臂仿真中，存在摩擦、外部扰动和阶跃操作输入时，同步约在2秒内实现，并揭示了精度与控制抖振之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 所提出的控制器显著减少了自适应参数，同时保证了系统稳定性和同步性能，为实际应用提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Bilateral teleoperation requires stability despite uncertain master and slave dynamics and delayed communication channels. Existing radial basis function neural network (RBFNN) controllers mainly differ in uncertainty decomposition, while online adaptive parameters often increase with network size. This paper proposes a compact two-sided adaptive controller for a nonlinear bilateral teleoperator with constant forward and backward delays. Operator and environment impedances are incorporated into the manipulator dynamics, and each side uses only two scalar adaptive estimates: one for the ideal RBF weight norm and another for the combined effects of friction, approximation error, and disturbances. Both estimates are updated through sigma-modification, resulting in only two adaptive parameters regardless of the number of RBF nodes. A Lyapunov-Krasovskii functional integrating sliding-variable energy, estimation errors, and delay-dependent integral terms is developed. Free-weighting matrices based on sliding-surface identities provide delay-dependent matrix conditions that guarantee uniform ultimate boundedness of synchronization errors, sliding variables, and adaptive estimates. Simulations on two 2-degree-of-freedom (2-DOF) revolute manipulators with friction, external disturbance, and stepwise operator inputs demonstrate synchronization within approximately 2 s and reveal the trade-off between accuracy and control chattering.

</details>


### [216] [Zero-Sum Power Factor Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20298)
*Cameron Khanpour,Samuel Talkington,Mathieu Dahan,Daniel K. Molzahn*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究电网中分布式能源（DER）的电压调节问题，提出一种鲁棒最小最大优化方法，通过选择DER的无功功率因数来最小化最坏情况下的电压偏差，并给出闭式解及数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式能源的主动功率注入波动（如设备行为或调度异常）会导致电压偏差，运营商需要在观测到功率注入前预先设定DER的无功参数（如IEEE 1547-2018中的恒定功率因数模式），以限制电压偏差。

Method: 采用线性电压模型，将运营商的决策建模为鲁棒最小最大问题，通过将功率因数决策表示为连续的无功-有功比，并依据电压偏差符号精确分解收益函数，进而求解。在电压残差始终位于初始侧的条件下，推导出闭式最小最大策略。

Result: 确定了该闭式策略适用的实际DER额定功率范围，量化了受限功率因数范围下损失的调节能力。数值测试验证了抵消计算，在代表性DER额定值下直接求解完整最小最大问题，并与非线性交流潮流对比验证了线性电压模型的准确性。

Conclusion: 所提出的鲁棒最小最大方法能有效选择DER功率因数以最小化最坏电压偏差，在特定条件下存在闭式解，且数值实验表明线性模型在预测电压方面具有实用性。

Abstract: Variable active power injections arising from device behavior or compromised dispatch complicate voltage regulation in electric power networks with distributed energy resources (DERs). An operator can limit the resulting voltage deviations by remotely selecting DER reactive power parameters before observing the active power injections. IEEE Standard 1547-2018 specifies constant power factor as one such control mode, coupling each device's reactive power to its realized active power. Using a linear voltage model, we formulate the operator's decision as a robust minimax problem in which the operator minimizes the largest feasible aggregate voltage deviation. We solve this problem by expressing the power factor decisions through continuous reactive to active power ratios and exactly decomposing the payoff according to the signs of the voltage deviations. When every feasible voltage residual remains on its initial side of nominal, the resulting ratios cancel each injection's contribution and yield a closed form minimax strategy. We identify realistic DER ratings for which this strategy applies and quantify the regulation capacity lost under restricted power factor ranges. Numerical tests check the cancellation computation, solve the complete minimax problem directly at a representative DER rating, and compare the linear voltage predictions with nonlinear AC power flow.

</details>


### [217] [Taming the Tilt: A Unified Pilot Control Concept for Transformational eVTOL Aircraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20300)
*Daniel Milz,Marc May,Andreas Seefried,Tobias Bellmann*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于eVTOL飞行器的导频控制系统设计，通过主动力反馈侧杆和命令滤波器实现多阶段飞行的无缝过渡，并验证了其在降低飞行员工作负荷方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: eVTOL飞行器因高效巡航和低基础设施依赖而受关注，但其多阶段飞行动力学差异大，控制设计复杂，若不加处理会增加飞行员负荷，因此需要开发降低负荷的导频控制系统。

Method: 采用串联倾转翼飞机仿真，在带有主动力反馈侧杆的全动模拟器上实现piloting控制概念，提供触觉反馈并利用自由度进行过渡，同时提出基于最优控制的方法评估命令滤波器性能。

Result: 命令滤波器相比闭环系统不显著增加任务时长，主动侧杆有助于减少操纵杆活动。

Conclusion: 提出的导频控制概念能有效支持多阶段飞行，降低飞行员负荷，并通过验证证明了其实用性。

Abstract: Transformational electric vertical take-off and landing (eVTOL) vehicles have gained significant attention over the past decade due to their efficient wing-borne cruise capabilities and reduced reliance on ground-based infrastructure. However, control system design for these vehicles remains challenging, as they must operate across multiple flight phases, each with distinct dominant dynamics. If left unaddressed, this complexity would significantly increase pilot workload, thus motivating the development of pilot control systems for multi-phase flight operations. The Simplified Vehicle Operations concept presents a promising strategy for reducing pilot workload. This study presents the design and implementation of a novel pilot control concept for eVTOL aircraft, validated through a tandem tilt-wing aircraft simulation on a full-motion simulator equipped with an active, force-feedback side stick. The system provides pilots with tactile feedback during specific flight phases, supporting intuitive control. The proposed approach enables seamless transitions and multi-phase flight maneuvers by leveraging the available degrees of freedom. Furthermore, an optimal-control-based methodology is proposed as a metric to evaluate command-filter-induced performance penalties and inceptor activities. The results show that the proposed command filter does not significantly increase the mission duration compared to the closed-loop system, while the active side stick helps reduce inceptor activity.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [218] [Deep Learning for Automated Quantification of Tumor-Associated Macrophages from H&E-Stained Slides in Diffuse Large B-Cell Lymphoma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19283)
*Anastasiia Studenikina,Svetlana Illarionova,Joaquim Carreras,Daniil Sulimov,Olga Filimonova,Dmitry Zvezdin,Arsenii Galimov,Ivan Tyukin,Rifat Hamoudi,Maxim Sharaev*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本研究探索了使用深度学习从常规HE染色切片中量化弥漫大B细胞淋巴瘤（DLBCL）中M2型肿瘤相关巨噬细胞（TAMs）的可行性，发现Swin-U-Net模型在分割性能上表现优异，且预测的CD163水平与预后相关，可作为IHC的潜在替代方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CD163免疫组化（IHC）在评估DLBCL中TAMs时资源密集、耗时，而M2型TAMs是疾病侵袭性的重要指标，因此需要一种可扩展、成本效益高的替代方案。

Method: 使用52例DLBCL患者的高分辨率HE图像和IHC验证的1,713个TAM实例，评估了U-Net、Swin-U-Net、Cerberus-U-Net3+、YOLOv11和HoVer-Net五种架构，并进行了生存分析和多变量Cox回归。

Result: 高CD163 TAM密度与显著降低的总生存期（HR 2.73）和无进展生存期（HR 2.88）相关；Swin-U-Net在分割精度（Precision 0.694, F1-score 0.633）上最优，Cerberus-U-Net3+灵敏度最高（Recall 0.656）；基于Swin-U-Net预测的CD163水平显示20.8%截断点，高预测值患者总生存期有下降趋势（HR 2.63）。

Conclusion: 深度学习架构（尤其是Swin-U-Net）在HE切片上量化TAMs方面显示出作为IHC替代方案的潜力，具有可扩展性和成本效益，有助于DLBCL的预后评估。

Abstract: While M2-polarized tumor-associated macrophages (TAMs) have been established as indicators of disease aggressiveness in diffuse large B-cell lymphoma (DLBCL), traditional CD163 immunohistochemistry (IHC) remains resource-intensive. This study aims to investigate the feasibility of using deep learning to quantify TAMs directly from standard hematoxylin and eosin-stained (HE) tissue sections. Using a curated dataset of 52 patients with DLBCL, with high-resolution HE images and IHC-validated annotations (1,713 TAM instances), five architectures were evaluated: U-Net, Swin-U-Net, Cerberus-U-Net3+, YOLOv11, and HoVer-Net. High CD163 TAM density (>20.04%) was associated with significantly reduced overall survival (HR 2.73; 95% CI:1.21-6.16; p=0.012) and progression-free survival (HR 2.88; 95% CI: 1.23-6.76; p=0.011). In multivariate Cox proportional hazards analysis adjusting for IPI, molecular subtype (GCB/non-GCB per Hans algorithm), EBV status, and age, CD163 TAM density showed a prognostic trend for overall survival (HR 3.24; 95% CI: 0.94-11.15; p=0.062) and progression-free survival (HR 2.41; 95% CI: 0.80-7.32; p=0.120). Among the evaluated models, the domain-specific Cerberus-U-Net3+ achieved the highest sensitivity (Recall 0.656), while the Transformer-based Swin-U-Net demonstrated superior segmentation fidelity (Precision 0.694, F1-score 0.633). Additionally, the survival analysis based on the predicted Swin-U-Net CD163 level revealed a 20.8% cutoff point for patients with high and low CD163 levels near IHC, as well as a downward trend in overall survival among patients with higher predicted CD163 values of 2.63 (95% CI: 0.85-8.33; p=0.083). These findings suggest that deep learning architectures using shifted-window self-attention show potential as a candidate surrogate for IHC that is scalable and cost-effective for prognostic assessment of TAMs in DLBCL.

</details>


### [219] [Loss-Resilient Semantic Communication over Packet-Loss Networks at Extreme-Low Bandwidth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19590)
*Shengshi Yao,Jincheng Dai,Sixian Wang,Guo Lu,Kai Niu,Wenjun Xu,Wenjun Zhang,Ping Zhang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出ResiGLC，一个面向极端低带宽丢包网络的鲁棒生成式潜码编码框架，利用语言模型的上下文预测能力和掩码学习策略，结合渐进式恢复解码，在保持极低码率的同时提升了丢包条件下的感知保真度和真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式语义编解码器仅优化压缩效率，对传输错误缺乏鲁棒性，在极端低带宽丢包网络中，高度紧凑的潜表示损坏会导致严重的保真度和真实性下降，且错误会跨潜在上下文和多步解码过程传播。

Method: 提出ResiGLC框架，利用生成与压缩的目标一致性，结合掩码学习策略，使模型支持任意上下文建模，从而缓解错误传播；在接收端设计渐进式恢复解码管道，分别利用潜在码的上下文关系和生成潜在空间中的多模态语义先验，联合优化压缩效率和丢包鲁棒性。

Result: 在丢包网络条件下，ResiGLC有效提升了感知保真度和真实感的鲁棒性，同时保持极低的带宽成本。

Conclusion: 通过联合优化压缩效率和丢包鲁棒性，ResiGLC在动态丢包情况下实现了优雅的性能退化，为极端低带宽鲁棒语义通信提供了有效方案。

Abstract: In extreme-low bandwidth network scenarios, generative semantic codecs have emerged as promising solutions to reduce bandwidth cost for visual communications. However, these learned codecs are usually optimized solely for compression efficiency and thus not robust against transmission errors. Corruptions due to packet-loss among these highly compact generative latent representations often cause more critical degradation in fidelity and realism, intensified by the severe error propagation across the latent contexts and multi-step decoding process. In this paper, we propose ResiGLC, a novel loss-resilient generative latent coding framework designed for robust semantic communication over extreme-low bandwidth packet-loss networks. Motivated by the inherent goal-consistency between generation and compression, we sufficiently exploit the impressive in-context predictive capabilities of language models. Integrated with the masked learning strategy, our model supports arbitrary context modeling of latent codes, which could mitigate the error propagation and handle unpredictable packet loss patterns. At the receiver, a progressive resilient decoding pipeline is presented, which leverages both the contextual relationship of the latent codes and the multi-modal semantic prior in the generative latent space, separately. By jointly optimizing toward both compression efficiency and packet-loss resilience, our proposed progressive decoding mechanism offers graceful performance when dealing with dynamic packet losses. Through extensive experimental evaluations, we establish that under packet-loss network conditions, ResiGLC can effectively improve the loss-resilience in terms of perceptual fidelity and realism qualities with extreme-low bandwidth cost.

</details>


### [220] [MOSAIC: A Self-supervised Dynamic Multi-encoding Reconstruction Framework for 3D Late Gadolinium Enhancement MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19506)
*Muhammad A. Sultan,Yingmin Liu,Katherine Binzel,Katarzyna E. Gil,Karolina M. Zareba,Rizwan Ahmad*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出并评估了一种用于高度欠采样的双回波三维晚期钆增强（3D LGE）MRI的自监督动态重建框架MOSAIC，能够在无需全采样训练数据或预计算灵敏度图的情况下，直接从欠采样数据中建模多回波图像内容、线圈灵敏度图和逐心跳非刚性运动，并在加速因子超过1000的条件下实现运动分辨的3D LGE成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D LGE MRI重建方法通常需要全采样训练数据或准确的预计算灵敏度图，且常将数据分箱到不同运动状态，可能损失细节或引入伪影。为突破这些限制，实现高度欠采样下的自由呼吸动态成像，研究者开发了MOSAIC。

Method: MOSAIC采用自监督方式，直接从未采样的数据中联合估计多回波图像、线圈灵敏度图以及每个心跳的特异性非刚性运动；与分箱或运动补偿方法不同，它逐心跳重建运动分辨的3D LGE图像。

Result: 在数字体模实验中，MOSAIC的峰值信噪比和结构相似性指数均优于低秩深度学习先验重建及MOSAIC的消融变体；在动物和人体研究中，其盲法专家图像质量评分优于内联图像导航压缩感知和低秩深度先验重建。

Conclusion: MOSAIC证明了在超过1000倍加速下进行运动分辨自由呼吸双回波3D LGE MRI的可行性，相比现有最先进方法，能更好地保留细节并抑制伪影。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop and evaluate a self-supervised dynamic reconstruction framework for highly undersampled dual-echo three-dimensional late gadolinium enhancement (3D LGE) MRI.
  Methods: MOSAIC jointly models multi-echo image content, coil sensitivity maps, and beat-specific nonrigid motion directly from acquired undersampled data, without requiring fully sampled training datasets or accurate precomputed sensitivity maps. Unlike existing methods that bin the acquired data into different motion states, with or without motion compensation, MOSAIC reconstructs a motion-resolved 3D LGE image from each heartbeat. The method was evaluated using digital phantoms with simulated myocardial scars and in vivo animal and human studies.
  Results: In phantom experiments, MOSAIC achieved higher peak signal-to-noise ratio and structural similarity index measure than low-rank deep image prior reconstruction and ablation variants of MOSAIC. In animal and human studies, MOSAIC achieved higher blinded expert image-quality scores than inline image-navigated compressed-sensing and low-rank deep image prior reconstructions.
  Conclusion: MOSAIC demonstrated the feasibility of motion-resolved free-breathing dual-echo 3D LGE MRI at acceleration factors exceeding 1,000, with improved detail preservation and artifact suppression relative to the state-of-the-art comparison methods.

</details>


### [221] [AsymFeX: A Symmetry-Driven Framework for Ischemic Stroke Segmentation Across Imaging Modalities and Stroke Stages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19769)
*Maunil Shah,Vaanathi Sundaresan*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于对称性驱动的两阶段3D分割方法用于急性缺血性卒中病灶分割，通过头位校正和跨半球特征提取模块，在AISD数据集上显著优于现有方法，并验证了跨模态泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非对比CT中梗死灶对比度低，手动勾画耗时费力，且临床常通过对比左右半球定位病灶，因此期望利用对称性信息实现快速准确的自动分割。

Method: 两阶段方法：第一阶段校正头部倾斜以对齐真实解剖中矢面；第二阶段使用新颖的不对称特征提取模块，通过跨半球注意力、特征差异估计和双尺度门控比较每个体素与其真实对侧对应体素，以捕获大小梗死灶。

Result: 在AISD数据集上，Dice达到0.6796，HD95为23.53mm，AVD为7.69mL，显著优于现有方法；在70mL溶栓阈值上具有临床相关体积分析；在ATLAS v2.1和ISLES'24上的概念验证表明跨模态和时间点泛化良好，不确定性分析支持临床部署可靠性。

Conclusion: 对称性驱动的设计有效提升AIS分割精度和泛化能力，有望辅助临床决策，代码已公开。

Abstract: Fast and accurate segmentation of Acute Ischemic Stroke (AIS) lesions is essential for stroke prognosis and treatment planning. Non-contrast CT (NCCT), the first-line imaging modality for diagnosing ischemic infarcts, exhibits subtle infarct contrast, making manual delineation slow and labor-intensive. Motivated by this, and by the clinical practice of comparing brain hemispheres to localize infarcts, we propose a two-stage, nnU-Net-compatible 3D segmentation method. The first stage corrects head tilt to align each scan to its true anatomical mid-sagittal plane; the second applies a novel Asymmetric Feature Extraction (AsymFeX) module, comparing each voxel to its true contralateral counterpart within a local 3 x 3 x 3 neighborhood via cross-hemispheric attention, feature disparity estimation, and dual-scale gating to capture both large and small infarcts. On AISD, our method achieves 0.6796 Dice, 23.53 mm HD95, and 7.69 mL AVD, significantly outperforming existing state-of-the-art methods, with clinically relevant volumetric analysis at the 70 mL thrombolysis-eligibility threshold. Proof-of-concept evaluation on ATLAS v2.1 and ISLES'24 demonstrates that the same symmetry-driven design generalizes across imaging modalities and stroke time points without architectural changes, further supported by an uncertainty analysis assessing reliability under clinical deployment. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/biomedia-lab/AIS-detection.

</details>


### [222] [MOSAIC: Modality-agnostic Spectral Alignment for Federated Image-level Weakly Supervised Tumor Segmentation under Client-specific Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19788)
*Tarun Kumar Garg,Vaanathi Sundaresan*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出MOSAIC，首个模态无关的联邦学习框架，用于在客户端特定缺失模态下进行弱监督肿瘤分割，通过模态对齐、频谱原型对齐和联邦细化网络，仅用图像级标签达到接近全监督的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机构临床环境中的多模态融合面临数据隐私和模态缺失问题，现有联邦学习无法有效处理客户端特定的异构缺失模态，且弱监督与联邦学习的结合尚未解决。

Method: 提出客户端特定模态对齐模块将可用模态融合到共享潜在空间，频谱原型对齐损失减少跨客户端分布偏移，联邦细化网络去噪CAM伪标签生成精确掩膜。

Result: 在FeTS2022、BraTS-MEN和BraTS-SSA三个基准上优于所有图像/框/点监督基线，FeTS2022上Dice达0.84，接近全监督，且新客户端加入无需重训，Dice损失仅0.01-0.04。

Conclusion: MOSAIC有效解决联邦弱监督下的模态缺失问题，突破弱监督精度上限，且支持动态客户端扩展，具有实际临床应用潜力。

Abstract: Trustworthy multimodal fusion in clinical settings requires handling incomplete and heterogeneous modality subsets across institutions, where privacy constraints prohibit centralized data sharing. Federated learning (FL) mitigates data-sharing constraints but suffers from client-specific missing modalities, where institutions possess incomplete multimodal subsets, degrading fusion quality and segmentation performance. While FL and weak supervision have been studied separately, their joint use with image-level labels under heterogeneous missing modalities remains unaddressed. We propose \textbf{MOSAIC}, the first modality-agnostic federated framework for weakly supervised binary tumor segmentation under client-specific missing modalities. We introduce a client-specific modality-alignment module that fuses available channels into a shared latent space without prior knowledge of modality identity, a spectral prototype alignment loss that reconciles cross-client distribution shift using compact non-invertible frequency-domain statistics, and a dedicated federated refinement network that denoises the resulting CAM pseudo-labels into accurate masks, breaking the accuracy ceiling of weak supervision. Experiments on three multi-institutional brain tumor benchmarks (FeTS2022, BraTS-MEN, and BraTS-SSA) demonstrate significant improvements over all image, box, and point-supervised baselines, approaching fully supervised accuracy using only image-level labels and reaching 0.84 Dice on FeTS2022. Dynamic new client addition enables previously unseen institutions to join an already-trained federation within 0.01-0.04 Dice without retraining. Code is available at https://github.com/Tarun2201/MOSAIC.

</details>


### [223] [Energy-Mamba: A Physics-Constrained State-Space Model for Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19813)
*Mohamed Mabrok,Yalda Zafari,Essam A. Rashed*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Energy-Mamba，通过可学习的势能函数将物理约束引入状态空间模型，以解决 Mamba 在医学图像中表示漂移的问题，在四个数据集上实现了更优的性能和更少的参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mamba 等状态空间模型在长程依赖上具有线性复杂度，但将其应用于 2D 图像时，无约束的状态演化会导致表示漂移，即动态隐藏状态逐渐失去对局部图像特征的保真度，这不利于医学图像中依赖细节的诊断。

Method: Energy-Mamba 引入可学习的势能函数，量化状态与局部特征的兼容性；通过自动微分计算梯度强制项，将状态拉向低能量构型，保持局部视觉保真度。该机制类似哈密顿动力学，将 SSM 扫描视为动能，约束视为势能，共同控制状态轨迹。

Result: 在四个医学图像数据集（视网膜 OCT、胸部 X 光、显微镜、腹部 CT）上，Energy-Mamba 以显著更少的参数达到了最先进的分类性能，验证了物理约束在提升效率和表示质量上的有效性。
